recyclerAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n                break;\n            case MOVED:\n                return;\n        }\n        saveScrollPosition();\n        onFragmentLoaded(false);\n    }\n\n    public void onEventMainThread(FeedItemEvent event) {\n        Log.d(TAG, "onEventMainThread() called with: " + "event = [" + event + "]");\n        if(queue == null || recyclerAdapter == null) {\n Here is a summary of the diagnosis and the patch.\n\n## Diagnosis\n\nThe report\'s key clue is: *"Empty view is removed and list is displayed. List contains an item (layout inspector), but it seems to be moved outside of the screen."*\n\nIn `QueueFragment.onFragmentLoaded()` the `RecyclerView` was set to `View.GONE` whenever the list became empty:\n\n```java\nif (queue == null || queue.size() == 0) {\n    recyclerView.setVisibility(View.GONE);\n    txtvEmpty.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n} else { ... }\n```\n\nSequence for the last remaining item:\n\n1. Swipe → `ItemTouchHelper` translates the row off-screen (`translationX = width`) and calls `onSwiped()`.\n2. `DBWriter.removeQueueItem()` → `QueueEvent.REMOVED` → `notifyItemRemoved(0)` and then `onFragmentLoaded()` hides the whole `RecyclerView`.\n3. A `GONE` `RecyclerView` is never measured/laid out by its parent, so the pending remove animation never completes: the swiped child is never detached, so `ItemTouchHelper`\'s pending cleanup (`Callback.clearView()`, which resets `translationX`/alpha) never runs.\n4. "Undo" → `QueueEvent.ADDED` → `notifyItemInserted(0)` + list made visible again. With stable IDs the still-attached, still-translated view holder is reused, so the restored item is laid out *off-screen* — exactly the reported symptom (empty view gone, list "empty" but containing one item).\n\n## Patch\n\n**`app/src/main/java/de/danoeh/antennapod/fragment/QueueFragment.java`**\n- `onFragmentLoaded()`: never hide the list; only toggle the empty view, which is declared after the `RecyclerView` in `queue_fragment.xml` (`match_parent`) and therefore covers it. This keeps layout passes running so item animations and `ItemTouchHelper` cleanup finish normally.\n- `loadItems()`: dropped the `recyclerView.setVisibility(View.GONE)` for the initial load as well (the progress bar is drawn over the empty list), so the list visibility is no longer toggled at all.\n- `onEventMainThread(QueueEvent)`: guard against `indexOfItemWithId() == -1` in the `REMOVED`/`IRREVERSIBLE_REMOVED` branch (previously `queue.remove(-1)` would throw if a removal event arrived for an item no longer in the local copy).\n\n**`app/src/main/java/de/danoeh/antennapod/adapter/QueueRecyclerAdapter.java`** and **`.../adapter/AllEpisodesRecycleAdapter.java`**\n- In `onBindViewHolder()`, reset `translationX`, `translationY` and `alpha` of the item view. This makes recycled views that were left in a swiped/dragged state recover on rebind, which also covers the Episodes → New / Favorites swipe-undo paths (their undo goes through `notifyItemChanged`/`notifyDataSetChanged`). `ItemTouchHelper` re-applies the translation of an actively swiped/dragged view on every draw frame, so ongoing gestures are unaffected.\n\nVerified with `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` — build succeeds (`app-free-debug.apk`, `app-play-debug.apk` produced).')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 837, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 59053, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 837, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 2, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHfVUAn78BVPURXi7vqx', stop_reason=None, session_id='7143df32-3438-4958-bb44-72d972d2b524', uuid='99d60e19-89cf-48b4-9ad5-676adf5d6e97')
